Let's understand operation:
input x: [B,N,N,C]
LayerNorm over last dim C: mean/var per (B,N,N). Then:
left_proj(x): [B,N,N,H]
right_proj(x): [B,N,N,H]
mask [B,N,N] broadcast over H: left *= mask, right *= mask.
left_gate = sigmoid(left_gate_proj(x)) [B,N,N,H]
right_gate etc
out_gate [B,N,N,H]
left = left * left_gate; right = right * right_gate.
out = sum over k: left[b,i,k,d] * right[b,j,k,d] for d in H. So for each b,i,j,out_channel_d = dot over k of left[b,i,k,d] and right[b,j,k,d]. This is batched per-channel product of matrices L_b [N,H] and R_b [N,H]: out[b,i,j,d] = sum_k L_b[i,k,d] R_b[j,k,d]. This can be seen for each d: out_d = L[:,d]^T R[:,d] (N x N). Or as einsum. Triton core batched GEMM:
Input:
L: [B, N, N, H] maybe bf16 contiguous. We need for each b,d compute L_d [N_i, N_k] and R_d [N_j,N_k], output [N_i,N_j]. In memory L[b,i,k,d], d contiguous. For fixed d, rows i stride N*H, k stride H, d stride 1. Strided K by H (128), not contiguous. GEMM load A tile of shape [BLOCK_I, BLOCK_K] with A_ptrs = L + b*... + i*stride_ik + k*stride_k + d. K stride = H (128). If H=128, loading 64 elements per k with stride 128 not contiguous, inefficient. Better transpose L to [B,H,N,N] contiguous (d batch first, then i,k) for GEMM: L_t[b,d,i,k] contiguous k. This copy costs memory but improves GEMM. torch.einsum likely does permute/copy. @triton.jit
def proj_kernel(X, Mask, LN_W, LN_B, WL, WG, WR, WG_R, Stats, Lt, Rt,
               B, M, C, H, eps,
               BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr,

   pid_b = tl.program_id(0)? Use grid (B, cdiv(M,BM)) or flat.
```
Flat grid maybe easier: total = B * cdiv(M,BM). `pid = tl.program_id(0); b = pid // num_m; pm = pid % num_m`.

Stats loop:
```
sum = zeros(BM) fp32
sumsq = zeros(BM)
for c in range(0, C, BC):
  cols = c + arange(BC)
  cmask = cols < C
  ptrs = X + b*M*C + offs_m[:,None]*C + cols[None,:]
  x = tl.load(ptrs, mask=mask_m[:,None] & cmask[None,:], other=0.0)
  sum += tl.sum(x, axis=1)
  sumsq += tl.sum(x*x, axis=1)
mean = tl.where(mask_m, sum / C, 0.0)
var = sumsq / C - mean*mean
var = tl.maximum(var, 0.0)
rstd = tl.where(mask_m, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + eps), 0.0)
# store stats
stats_ptrs = Stats + b*M*2 + offs_m
tl.store(stats_ptrs, mean, mask=mask_m)
tl.store(stats_ptrs+1, rstd, mask=mask_m)
```
Need total C float for division. C runtime int; fp conversion? `C.to(tl.float32)`? In Triton, Python int okay? Use `c_float = C.to(tl.float32)`? C is tl? We can pass `Cf` float? Or use `C` int in division, Triton casts. fine.

   # For cols>=C, x=0, mean/rstd maybe 0, lnw=0 -> xn=0.

   w_gl = ...

   w_gr ...

   acc_gl += tl.dot(xb, w_gl)
   acc_r += tl.dot(xb, w_r)

Outgate kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def outgate_kernel(X, Stats, LN_W, LN_B, WG, GateT, B,M,C,H,eps, BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C):
  pid flat; b,pm; offs_m; mask_m.
  mean = load stats[m]; rstd load stats[m+1]
  acc = zeros(BM,H)
  for c chunks:
    x load
    lnw,lnb load
    xn = (x - mean[:,None])*rstd[:,None]*lnw + lnb
    xb = xn.to(bf16)
    w = load WG chunk [BC,H]
    acc += dot(xb,w)
  gate = 1/(1+exp(-acc))
  gate_b = gate.to(bf16)
  gate_t = tl.trans(gate_b)
  store GateT base + h*M + m
```
Could avoid computing stats in proj kernel by outgate computing from stats. Good.

But note: Proj kernel computes LN stats with biased variance. Outgate uses same stats. This matches using same LN as PyTorch. Good.

Potential accuracy: If C=384, BC=128, weight chunks. Weight LN_w loaded per chunk. For columns beyond C in last chunk, x masked 0, mean/rstd row valid, lnw mask 0, lnb mask 0 -> xn=0. Dot weights masked 0. Good.

Final kernel:
Inputs out_t [B,H,M] bf16, gate_t [B,H,M] bf16, W_out [H,C] bf16 (shape [H,C] contiguous? Actually to_out weight transposed [H,C]); LN weight/bias H fp32. Output [B,M,C] fp32.
Grid: B * cdiv(M, BM_final) maybe c chunks? We'll process BM_FINAL rows, loop C chunks to store.
Parameters: BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C maybe 128. H=128.
Algorithm:
```
pid flat -> b, pm
offs_m = pm*BM + arange(BM); mask_m
h_offs = arange(0,H)
base = b*H*M
out_ptrs = OutT + base + h_offs[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:]
gate_ptrs = GateT + base + h_offs[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:]
# load tile [H,BM]
valid = (h_offs < H)[:,None] & mask_m[None,:] # H exact
out = tl.load(out_ptrs, mask=valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
gate = tl.load(gate_ptrs, mask=valid, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
# layer norm over h axis 0
mean = tl.sum(out, axis=0) / H
sumsq = tl.sum(out*out, axis=0)
var = sumsq/H - mean*mean; rstd = 1/sqrt(var+eps)
# normalize: out [H,BM]; mean [BM]
lnw = tl.load(ln_w + h_offs).to(fp32); lnb ...
x = (out - mean[None,:]) * rstd[None,:] * lnw[:,None] + lnb[None,:]
x = x * gate
# For dot, need [BM,H]
x_mh = tl.trans(x).to(tl.bfloat16)
# loop c chunks:
for c in range(0, C, BC):
   c_offs = c + arange(BC); cmask = c_offs<C
   w = tl.load(W + h_offs[:,None]*C + c_offs[None,:], mask=(h_offs<H)[:,None] & cmask[None,:], other=0.0) # bf16 [H,BC]
   acc = tl.dot(x_mh, w) # x [BM,H] bf16, w [H,BC] bf16 -> [BM,BC]
   store output at b*M*C + offs_m[:,None]*C + c_offs[None,:], mask=mask_m & cmask
```

Host custom_kernel:
```
def custom_kernel(data):
    input_tensor, mask, weights, config = data
    B,N,_,C = input_tensor.shape
    H = config['hidden_dim']
    M = N*N
    eps = config.get('epsilon', 1e-5)
    x = input_tensor.contiguous()
    if x.dtype != torch.float32: x = x.to(torch.float32)
    x = x.view(B,M,C)
    has_mask = (mask is not None) and not config.get('nomask', False)
    if has_mask:
        mask = mask.to(torch.float32).contiguous()
        if mask.shape != (B,N,N): ...
        mask = mask.view(B,M)
    else:
        mask = torch.empty(0, device=x.device, dtype=torch.float32)
    # weights
    ln_w = weights['norm.weight'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    ln_b = weights['norm.bias'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    def tbf16(w): return w.t().to(torch.bfloat16).contiguous()
    wl = tbf16(weights['left_proj.weight'])
    wr = tbf16(weights['right_proj.weight'])
    wgl = tbf16(weights['left_gate.weight'])
    wgr = tbf16(weights['right_gate.weight'])
    wog = tbf16(weights['out_gate.weight'])
    wout = tbf16(weights['to_out.weight'])
    # maybe to_out weight needs [H,C], if C maybe 128; wout shape C? Wait tbf16 does w.t(), for to_out weight shape [C,H] -> [H,C]. Good.
    # For gates: weight [H,C] -> [C,H]."
    bf16 = torch.bfloat16
    Lt = torch.empty((B,H,M), dtype=bf16, device=x.device)
    Rt = torch.empty(...)
    GateT = torch.empty(...)
    OutT = torch.empty(...)
    Stats = torch.empty((B,M,2), dtype=torch.float32, device=x.device)
    Out = torch.empty((B,N,N,C), dtype=torch.float32, device=x.device)
    # block sizes
    BLOCK_M_PROJ = 64
    BLOCK_C = 128 if C >= 128 else triton.next_power_of_2(C)
    NUM_C = triton.cdiv(C, BLOCK_C)
    H_BLOCK = triton.next_power_of_2(H)
    grid_proj = (B * triton.cdiv(M, BLOCK_M_PROJ),)
    proj_kernel[grid_proj](x, mask if has_mask else x, ln_w, ln_b, wl, wr, wgl, wgr, Stats, Lt, Rt,
                           B, M, C, H, eps,
                           BLOCK_M=BLOCK_M_PROJ, BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C, NUM_C=NUM_C, H_BLOCK=H_BLOCK, HAS_MASK=has_mask,
                           num_warps=8, num_stages=2)
    # outgate
    BLOCK_M_OUT = 128
    grid_outgate = (B * triton.cdiv(M, BLOCK_M_OUT),)
    outgate_kernel[...]...
    # core
    if N <= 256: BI=BN=64; BK=64; W=4; S=3
    else: BI=BN=128; BK=64; W=8; S=3
    num_i = triton.cdiv(N,BI); num_j=...
    grid_core = (B*H*num_i*num_j,)
    core_kernel[grid_core](Lt,Rt,OutT,N,H,M, BI, BN, BK, EVEN=(N%BI==0 and N%BN==0 and N%BK==0), num_warps=W, num_stages=S)
    # final
    BMF=64; BCF=128 if C>=128 else next_pow2(C); NUM_CF=...
    grid_final = (B*triton.cdiv(M,BMF),)
    final_kernel[...](OutT, GateT, wout, ln_w_out? to_out_norm weight/bias, Out, B,M,C,H,eps, BMF, BCF, NUM_CF, H_BLOCK, EVEN_C? num_warps=8, num_stages=2)
    return Out
```

Core kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def _core_kernel(L_ptr,R_ptr,O_ptr,B,N,H,M, BLOCK_I,BLOCK_J,BLOCK_K, EVEN: tl.constexpr):
    pid = tl.program_id(0)
    num_i = tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK_I)
    num_j = tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK_J)
    num_ij = num_i * num_j
    bh = pid // num_ij
    tile = pid % num_ij
    b = bh // H
    h = bh % H
    # n fastest
    i = tile // num_j
    j = tile - i * num_j
    offs_i = i*BLOCK_I + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_I)
    offs_j = j*BLOCK_J + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_J)
    offs_k = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_K)
    base = (b*H + h) * M
    l_ptrs = L_ptr + base + offs_i[:,None]*N + offs_k[None,:]
    r_ptrs = R_ptr + base + offs_j[:,None]*N + offs_k[None,:]
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_I,BLOCK_J), dtype=tl.float32)
    if EVEN:
       for k in range(0, N, BLOCK_K):
          a = tl.load(l_ptrs)
          bmat = tl.load(r_ptrs)
          acc += tl.dot(a, tl.trans(bmat))
          l_ptrs += BLOCK_K
          r_ptrs += BLOCK_K
    else:
       i_mask = offs_i < N
       j_mask = offs_j < N
       ij_mask = i_mask[:,None] & j_mask[None,:]
       nk = tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK_K)
       for kk in range(0, nk):
          k = kk * BLOCK_K
          k_offs = k + offs_k
          k_mask = k_offs < N
          amask = i_mask[:,None] & k_mask[None,:]
          jmask = j_mask[:,None] & k_mask[None,:]
          a = tl.load(l_ptrs, mask=amask, other=0.0)
          bmat = tl.load(r_ptrs, mask=jmask, other=0.0) # ptrs currently point at k start? Need if using k offsets absolute.
```
If using pointer increments in EVEN, for non-EVEN easier use absolute k offset each loop:
```
for kk in range(0, tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK_K)):
  k_offs = kk*BLOCK_K + offs_k
  a_ptrs = L_ptr + base + offs_i[:,None]*N + k_offs[None,:]
  b_ptrs = R_ptr + base + offs_j[:,None]*N + k_offs[None,:]
```
This recomputes pointers but okay. For EVEN also can use pointer increments. To keep one code path maybe use absolute with no mask if EVEN:
```

    else:
       masks...
```

Or pass NUM_K runtime. Use pointer increment for speed. Let's implement two paths with pointer increments only for EVEN (tests). For non-EVEN, pointer increments and mask with k offset. Need maintain `k` value. Dynamic loop variable `k = 0; for _ in range(num_k):`? In Triton, can do:
```

    ...
    l_ptrs += BLOCK_K
```
For non-even, compute `k_mask = (k + offs_k) < N` where k is loop var. Since l_ptrs starts at k=0 and increments, okay.

```

  for k in range(0,N,BLOCK_K): ...
else:
  i_mask=...
  for k in range(0,N,BLOCK_K):
      k_mask = (k + offs_k) < N

      bm = tl.load(r_ptrs, mask=j_mask[:,None] & k_mask[None,:], other=0.0)

      l_ptrs += BLOCK_K; r_ptrs += BLOCK_K
# store

else: tl.store(o_ptrs, acc.to(tl.bfloat16), mask=ij_mask, other=0)
```

Final kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(OUTT_PTR, GATE_PTR, W_OUT_PTR, LN_W2_PTR, LN_B2_PTR, OUT_PTR,
                  B, M, C, H, EPS, NUM_M,
                  BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr, H_BLOCK: tl.constexpr, EVEN_C: tl.constexpr):
   pid = tl.program_id(0)
   b = pid // NUM_M
   pm = pid % NUM_M
   offs_m = pm*BLOCK_M + arange(BM); row_mask=offs_m<M
   h_offs=arange(HB); h_mask=h_offs<H
   base=b*H*M
   load_mask = h_mask[:,None]&row_mask[None,:]
   out = tl.load(OUTT_PTR + base + h_offs[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
   gate = tl.load(GATE_PTR + base + h_offs[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
   mean = tl.sum(out, axis=0) / H  # H actual, padded zeros ok
   sumsq = tl.sum(out*out, axis=0)
   var = tl.maximum(sumsq / H - mean*mean, 0.0)
   rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var+EPS), 0.0)
   lnw = tl.load(LN_W2_PTR + h_offs, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
   lnb = tl.load(LN_B2_PTR + h_offs, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
   x = (out - mean[None,:]) * rstd[None,:] * lnw[:,None] + lnb[None,:]
   x = x * gate
   # For invalid rows, rstd=0 so x=lnb? Actually if row invalid, rstd=0, mean=0, out=0, x=lnb for valid h? Wait invalid row mask not applied to out load, other=0. rstd set 0. x = (0-0)*0*lnw + lnb = lnb, not zero! Then multiplied by gate (0) -> 0. Good. If gate other=0, x becomes 0. OK.
   x_b = x.to(bf16)
   x_mh = tl.trans(x_b)  # [BM,H_BLOCK]
   c_base = arange(BC)
   for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
       c_offs = cb*BC + c_base
       if EVEN_C? If C not multiple BC, need c_mask for load/store; if multiple, no.
       w_mask = h_mask[:,None] & (c_offs<C)[None,:]
       w = tl.load(W_OUT_PTR + h_offs[:,None]*C + c_offs[None,:], mask=w_mask, other=0.0) # W_OUT [H,C]
       acc = tl.dot(x_mh, w)
       store_mask = row_mask[:,None] & (c_offs<C)[None,:]
       tl.store(OUT_PTR + b*M*C + offs_m[:,None]*C + c_offs[None,:], acc, mask=store_mask)
```
Need W_OUT shape [H,C], actual H,C. h_mask for loaded w. If H_BLOCK>H, padded h rows zero. Dot K=H_BLOCK, `x_mh` has H_BLOCK columns. w has H_BLOCK rows. Good.

But `x_mh = tl.trans(x_b)` where x_b shape [H_BLOCK, BLOCK_M]. `tl.trans` -> [BLOCK_M,H_BLOCK]. Good.

Potential: In final, for invalid rows, rstd=0 but x = lnb*gate. gate=0, so x=0. Good.

Output `acc` fp32. We may want to set invalid c to 0; store masked.

EVEN_C: if C % BLOCK_C ==0, no c mask? H_mask still. We can simplify by always using masks for c; performance small. Use always mask to support. For C=128, BC=128 mask all true but compiler doesn't know; could cost. We can pass EVEN_C and branch. But final small; always mask okay? `c_offs < C` compute and mask store; store mask all true but still uses predicated stores. Might be okay. For core, masks matter. For final, use branch to avoid overhead? Could. Use `EVEN_C: tl.constexpr`; in loop:
```
if EVEN_C:
  w = tl.load(W_OUT_PTR + h_offs[:,None]*C + c_offs[None,:], mask=h_mask[:,None], other=0.0)
  acc = dot
  tl.store(OUT_PTR + ..., acc, mask=row_mask[:,None])
else: ... c_mask
```
But `h_mask` needed if H_BLOCK > H. For H=128, h_mask all true; could omit but keep. To reduce code, use mask always. Simpler.

C chunks `NUM_C` maybe 1/3. `c_offs` computed.

Let's draft code in mind.

```
import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl

def _next_pow2(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()

@triton.jit
def _sigmoid(x):
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-x))
```
In `tl.exp` for fp32; okay.

Proj kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def _proj_kernel(X_ptr, MASK_ptr, LN_W_ptr, LN_B_ptr,
                 WL_ptr, WR_ptr, WGL_ptr, WGR_ptr,
                 STATS_ptr, LT_ptr, RT_ptr,
                 B, M, C, H, EPS,
                 BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr, H_BLOCK: tl.constexpr,
                 HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr):
    b = tl.program_id(0)
    pm = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m = pm * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    row_mask = offs_m < M
    c_base = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    h_offs = tl.arange(0, H_BLOCK)
    h_mask = h_offs < H
    sum_x = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
    sum_sq = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
    for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
        c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
        cmask = c_offs < C
        mask2d = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
        x = tl.load(X_ptr + b * M * C + offs_m[:, None] * C + c_offs[None, :], mask=mask2d, other=0.0)
        sum_x += tl.sum(x, axis=1)
        sum_sq += tl.sum(x * x, axis=1)
    mean = tl.where(row_mask, sum_x / C, 0.0)
    var = tl.maximum(sum_sq / C - mean * mean, 0.0)
    rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS), 0.0)
    stats_offs = STATS_ptr + b * M * 2 + offs_m
    tl.store(stats_offs, mean, mask=row_mask)
    tl.store(stats_offs + 1, rstd, mask=row_mask)

    acc_l = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_BLOCK), dtype=tl.float32)
    acc_gl = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_BLOCK), dtype=tl.float32)
    acc_r = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_BLOCK), dtype=tl.float32)
    acc_gr = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_BLOCK), dtype=tl.float32)
    for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
        c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
        cmask = c_offs < C
        mask2d = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
        x = tl.load(X_ptr + b * M * C + offs_m[:, None] * C + c_offs[None, :], mask=mask2d, other=0.0)
        lnw = tl.load(LN_W_ptr + c_offs, mask=cmask, other=0.0)
        lnb = tl.load(LN_B_ptr + c_offs, mask=cmask, other=0.0)
        xn = (x - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * lnw[None, :] + lnb[None, :]
        xn = tl.where(row_mask[:, None], xn, 0.0)
        xb = xn.to(tl.bfloat16)
        w_mask = cmask[:, None] & h_mask[None, :]
        w_l = tl.load(WL_ptr + c_offs[:, None] * H + h_offs[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
        w_gl = tl.load(WGL_ptr + c_offs[:, None] * H + h_offs[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
        w_r = tl.load(WR_ptr + c_offs[:, None] * H + h_offs[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
        w_gr = tl.load(WGR_ptr + c_offs[:, None] * H + h_offs[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
        acc_l += tl.dot(xb, w_l)
        acc_gl += tl.dot(xb, w_gl)
        acc_r += tl.dot(xb, w_r)
        acc_gr += tl.dot(xb, w_gr)
    gl = _sigmoid(acc_gl)
    gr = _sigmoid(acc_gr)
    if HAS_MASK:
        m_mask = tl.load(MASK_ptr + b * M + offs_m, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)
        l_val = acc_l * gl * m_mask[:, None]
        r_val = acc_r * gr * m_mask[:, None]
    else:
        l_val = acc_l * gl
        r_val = acc_r * gr
    l_b = l_val.to(tl.bfloat16)
    r_b = r_val.to(tl.bfloat16)
    l_t = tl.trans(l_b)
    r_t = tl.trans(r_b)
    store_mask = h_mask[:, None] & row_mask[None, :]
    base_t = b * H * M
    lt_ptrs = LT_ptr + base_t + h_offs[:, None] * M + offs_m[None, :]
    rt_ptrs = RT_ptr + base_t + h_offs[:, None] * M + offs_m[None, :]
    tl.store(lt_ptrs, l_t, mask=store_mask)
    tl.store(rt_ptrs, r_t, mask=store_mask)
```
Potential bug: `mask2d = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]` where row_mask shape [BM], cmask [BC]. OK.
`x * x` for masked out-of-row x=0. Good.
`mean = sum_x / C`: C int. If C=0? no.
`tl.where(row_mask, sum_x / C, 0.0)` row_mask bool [BM], sum_x/C fp32 [BM], 0 float. OK.
`acc_l += tl.dot` with acc_l fp32, dot fp32? Dot inputs bf16, default output dtype? In Triton, tl.dot returns float32 if inputs bf16. Good.
`w_mask` shape [BC,H_BLOCK]. We load four weight tiles each iteration; h_mask repeated. OK.
Store `base_t = b * H * M`; H runtime, M runtime; int. `h_offs[:, None] * M` where h_offs H_BLOCK; for h invalid, masked. Pointer for invalid h maybe points beyond base by >H*M; masked. OK.

Outgate kernel similar with one acc:
```
@triton.jit
def _outgate_kernel(X_ptr, STATS_ptr, LN_W_ptr, LN_B_ptr, WG_ptr, GATE_PTR,
                    B, M, C, H, EPS,
                    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr, H_BLOCK: tl.constexpr):
  b=tl.program_id(0); pm=tl.program_id(1)
  offs_m...
  row_mask...
  mean = tl.load(STATS_ptr + b*M*2 + offs_m, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)
  rstd = tl.load(STATS_ptr + b*M*2 + offs_m + 1, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)
  acc = zeros
  for cb...
    x load, lnw,lnb, xn, where row, xb, w, acc += dot
  gate = _sigmoid(acc)
  gate_b = gate.to(bf16)
  gate_t = tl.trans(gate_b)
  store_mask = h_mask[:,None]&row_mask[None,:]
  store GATE_PTR + b*H*M + h*M + m
```
Need B param? For grid B not used inside? b from pid, B not needed. But base uses H*M. B not used. Can omit B from kernel? For 2D grid, b range known, base b*H*M. No B. But for pointer X `b*M*C`, no B. So B param unnecessary. Keep maybe unused? Triton may warn? It's okay. We can omit to reduce. For stats, b. Grid provides. Use no B.

Projection kernel also doesn't need B param. For dummy mask, no B. Remove B from kernels except maybe not. In host, grid dims B. Inside b from pid. Good.

Core kernel needs B? b from bh // H. No B. H needed. M,N.

Final kernel no B.

Now, core kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def _core_kernel(L_ptr, R_ptr, O_ptr, N, H, M, BLOCK_I: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_J: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_K: tl.constexpr, EVEN: tl.constexpr):
    j = tl.program_id(0)
    i = tl.program_id(1)
    bh = tl.program_id(2)
    b = bh // H
    h = bh % H
    offs_i = i*BLOCK_I + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_I)
    offs_j = j*BLOCK_J + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_J)
    offs_k = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_K)
    base = (b*H + h) * M
    l_ptrs = L_ptr + base + offs_i[:,None]*N + offs_k[None,:]
    r_ptrs = R_ptr + base + offs_j[:,None]*N + offs_k[None,:]
    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_I,BLOCK_J), dtype=tl.float32)
    if EVEN:
       for k in range(0, N, BLOCK_K):
          a = tl.load(l_ptrs)
          bm = tl.load(r_ptrs)
          acc += tl.dot(a, tl.trans(bm))
          l_ptrs += BLOCK_K
          r_ptrs += BLOCK_K
    else:
       i_mask = offs_i < N
       j_mask = offs_j < N
       for k in range(0, N, BLOCK_K):
          k_mask = (k + offs_k) < N
          a = tl.load(l_ptrs, mask=i_mask[:,None] & k_mask[None,:], other=0.0)
          bm = tl.load(r_ptrs, mask=j_mask[:,None] & k_mask[None,:], other=0.0)
          acc += tl.dot(a, tl.trans(bm))
          l_ptrs += BLOCK_K
          r_ptrs += BLOCK_K
    o_ptrs = O_ptr + base + offs_i[:,None]*N + offs_j[None,:]
    o_val = acc.to(tl.bfloat16)
    if EVEN:
       tl.store(o_ptrs, o_val)
    else:
       i_mask = offs_i < N
       j_mask = offs_j < N
       tl.store(o_ptrs, o_val, mask=i_mask[:,None]&j_mask[None,:], other=0.0)
```
In non-even, l_ptrs initially at k=0. In each iteration, it loads current k block. After load, pointer increments by BLOCK_K. `k` loop variable also increments. Good. However for non-even, if N not multiple BLOCK_I, l_ptrs for invalid rows at k=0 may be beyond allocated but masked. Increment pointer for invalid rows still okay. Store masked.
Potential issue: In EVEN path, no mask, if N not multiple but EVEN false. Good.
`for k in range(0, N, BLOCK_K)`: N runtime. In Triton, range with step constexpr? BLOCK_K constexpr, N int. Should work.

But pointer increment `l_ptrs += BLOCK_K` increments each element? `l_ptrs` is a tensor of pointers shape [BI,BK]. Adding scalar BLOCK_K adds to all pointers, equivalent k+1 because stride_k=1. Good. `r_ptrs += BLOCK_K` same.

However, for B tile R_t [j,k] row-major, pointer offset j*N + k. Incrementing by 1 in k. Good.

Now, if BLOCK_K=64, N=1024, 16 iterations. `k` loop variable int. In non-even, k_mask uses `k + offs_k`. Good.

Now, final kernel:
```
@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(OUTT_PTR, GATE_PTR, W_OUT_PTR, LN_W_PTR, LN_B_PTR, OUT_PTR,
                  M, C, H, EPS,
                  BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr, H_BLOCK: tl.constexpr):
    b = tl.program_id(0)
    pm = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m = pm*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_M)
    row_mask = offs_m < M
    h_offs = tl.arange(0,H_BLOCK)
    h_mask = h_offs < H
    c_base = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_C)
    base = b*H*M
    load_mask = h_mask[:,None] & row_mask[None,:]
    out = tl.load(OUTT_PTR + base + h_offs[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    gate = tl.load(GATE_PTR + base + h_offs[:,None]*M + offs_m[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
    mean = tl.sum(out, axis=0) / H
    sum_sq = tl.sum(out * out, axis=0)
    var = tl.maximum(sum_sq / H - mean * mean, 0.0)
    rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS), 0.0)
    lnw = tl.load(LN_W_PTR + h_offs, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
    lnb = tl.load(LN_B_PTR + h_offs, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
    x = (out - mean[None, :]) * rstd[None, :] * lnw[:, None] + lnb[None, :]
    x = x * gate
    x_b = x.to(tl.bfloat16)
    x_mh = tl.trans(x_b)  # [BLOCK_M, H_BLOCK]
    for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
        c_offs = cb*BLOCK_C + c_base
        cmask = c_offs < C
        w_mask = h_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
        w = tl.load(W_OUT_PTR + h_offs[:, None] * C + c_offs[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
        acc = tl.dot(x_mh, w)
        store_mask = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
        tl.store(OUT_PTR + b * M * C + offs_m[:, None] * C + c_offs[None, :], acc, mask=store_mask)
```
Denominator `H` int. If H not exact, padded zeros. For invalid rows, rstd=0, x=(0-0)*0*lnw+lnb=lnb, gate=0, x*gate=0. good.
`acc` fp32 [BM,BC], store float32. For out-of-range c masked.

Potential compile: `tl.dot(x_mh, w)` where x_mh [BLOCK_M,H_BLOCK], w [H_BLOCK,BLOCK_C]. If BLOCK_C maybe 128, H_BLOCK 128. Good. If C<BC, w masked zero for invalid c. Dot still K=H_BLOCK.

Now, maybe final LN should use `H` float but if H is Python int, division produces float? If H int, sum fp32 / int -> fp32. Good.

Now, host conversions. Need be careful with `to_out_norm.weight` name. The weight dict keys exactly: 'norm.weight', 'norm.bias', 'left_proj.weight', ... 'to_out_norm.weight', 'to_out_norm.bias', 'to_out.weight'. Use.

Host code:
```
def custom_kernel(data):
    input_tensor, mask, weights, config = data
    B, N, _, C_in = input_tensor.shape
    H = int(config['hidden_dim'])
    C = C_in
    M = N * N
    dev = input_tensor.device
    x = input_tensor.contiguous()
    if x.dtype != torch.float32:
        x = x.to(torch.float32)
    x = x.view(B, M, C)
    eps = config.get('epsilon', 1e-5)
    has_mask = mask is not None and not config.get('nomask', False)
    if has_mask:
       mask = mask.to(torch.float32).contiguous()
       if mask.shape != (B,N,N):
           if mask.shape == (N,N) and B == 1:
               mask = mask.unsqueeze(0)
           elif mask.ndim == 3 and mask.shape[0] == 1 and B > 1:
               mask = mask.expand(B,N,N)
           else:
               mask = mask.view(B,N,N) # may fail
       mask = mask.view(B,M).contiguous()
    else:
       mask = x  # dummy
    ln_w = weights['norm.weight'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    ln_b = weights['norm.bias'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    def tbf16(w): return w.t().to(torch.bfloat16).contiguous()
    wl = tbf16(weights['left_proj.weight'])
    wr = tbf16(weights['right_proj.weight'])
    wgl = tbf16(weights['left_gate.weight'])
    wgr = tbf16(weights['right_gate.weight'])
    wog = tbf16(weights['out_gate.weight'])
    wout = tbf16(weights['to_out.weight'])
    ln_w2 = weights['to_out_norm.weight'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    ln_b2 = weights['to_out_norm.bias'].to(torch.float32).contiguous()

    bf16 = torch.bfloat16
    Lt = torch.empty((B, H, M), dtype=bf16, device=dev)
    Rt = torch.empty((B, H, M), dtype=bf16, device=dev)
    GateT = torch.empty((B, H, M), dtype=bf16, device=dev)
    OutT = torch.empty((B, H, M), dtype=bf16, device=dev)
    Stats = torch.empty((B, M, 2), dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)
    Out = torch.empty((B, N, N, C), dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)

    H_BLOCK = _next_pow2(H)
    if C >= 128:
       BLOCK_C = 128
    else:
       BLOCK_C = _next_pow2(C)
    NUM_C = triton.cdiv(C, BLOCK_C)
    # Ensure BLOCK_C is power and >=16? If C<16, dot K small maybe <16. C >=128. But robust: if BLOCK_C < 16: BLOCK_C=16? Could if C small. We can set BLOCK_C = max(16, _next_pow2(min(C,128))? Hmm. For C=128, 128.
    if BLOCK_C < 16: BLOCK_C = 16; NUM_C=triton.cdiv(C,16)
```
For C=1? Not tests. If BLOCK_C=16 and C=1, cmask; dot K=16. Good.

But if C=384, BLOCK_C=128, NUM_C=3. If C=768, NUM_C=6. Good.

Now, let's rewrite final kernel accordingly. This is the most complex. Pseudocode:
```
@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(..., H_CHUNK, NUM_H):
    b = pid0; pm=pid1
    offs_m_raw...
    row_mask, m_offs
    h_base = tl.arange(0,H_CHUNK)
    c_base = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_C)
    base = b*H*M
    if NUM_H == 1:
        h_offs = h_base
        h_mask = h_offs < H
        h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)
        load_mask = h_mask[:,None] & row_mask[None,:]
        out = load(OUTT + base + h_pad[:,None]*M + m_offs[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0).to(f32)
        gate = load(GATE + ...)
        mean = sum(out,axis=0)/H
        sum_sq = sum(out*out,axis=0)
        var...
        rstd = where(row_mask,...)
        lnw = load(LN_W + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0)
        lnb = ...
        x = (out - mean[None,:])*rstd[None,:]*lnw[:,None]+lnb[None,:]
        x = x * gate
        x_mh = tl.trans(x.to(bf16))
        for cb in static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs...
            c_pad...
            cmask...
            w_mask = h_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
            w = load(W_PTR + h_pad[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], mask=w_mask, other=0)
            acc = tl.dot(x_mh, w)
            store_mask = row_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
            tl.store(OUT_PTR + b*M*C + m_offs[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], acc, mask=store_mask)
    else:
        # stats pass
        sum_x = zeros(BM); sum_sq=zeros(BM)
        for hb in static_range(NUM_H):
            h_offs = hb*H_CHUNK + h_base
            h_mask = h_offs < H
            h_pad = where(...)
            load_mask = h_mask[:,None] & row_mask[None,:]
            out = load(...).to(f32)
            sum_x += tl.sum(out, axis=0)
            sum_sq += tl.sum(out*out, axis=0)
        mean = sum_x / H
        var = sum_sq/H - mean*mean
        rstd = where(row_mask,...)
        # compute pass
        for cb in static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs... c_pad... cmask...
            acc = zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), f32)
            # ln weight/bias? Could load inside h because h chunk differs.
            for hb in static_range(NUM_H):
                h_offs = ...
                h_mask = ...
                h_pad = ...
                load_mask = ...
                out = load(...)
                gate = load(...)
                lnw = load(LN_W + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0)
                lnb = ...
                x = (out - mean[None,:])*rstd[None,:]*lnw[:,None]+lnb[None,:]
                x = x * gate
                x_mh = tl.trans(x.to(bf16))
                w_mask = h_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
                w = load(W_PTR + h_pad[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], mask=w_mask, other=0)
                acc = acc + tl.dot(x_mh, w)
            store_mask = row_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
            tl.store(OUT_PTR + b*M*C + m_offs[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], acc, mask=store_mask)
```
This works. For NUM_H==1, we avoid reloading out/gate for each c; x_mh computed once. For C chunks, w loaded per c. Good.

In general branch, for each c, we reload out/gate for each h. If C multiple 3, out/gate read 3x. Could optimize by h outer and c inner with output accumulation array [BM,C_total]? But C_total maybe <=768, BM=64 -> 64*768*4=196KB too high. Not. Current okay.

Now, update `_proj_kernel` signature with `H_CHUNK: tl.constexpr, NUM_H: tl.constexpr`. Remove `H_BLOCK`.

Let's write projection carefully:
```
@triton.jit
def _proj_kernel(X_ptr, MASK_ptr, LN_W_ptr, LN_B_ptr,
                 WL_ptr, WR_ptr, WGL_ptr, WGR_ptr,
                 STATS_ptr, LT_ptr, RT_ptr,
                 M, C, H, EPS,
                 BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr,
                 H_CHUNK: tl.constexpr, NUM_H: tl.constexpr,
                 HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr):
    b = tl.program_id(0); pm = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m_raw = pm*BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0,BLOCK_M)
    row_mask = offs_m_raw < M
    m_offs = tl.where(row_mask, offs_m_raw, 0)
    c_base = tl.arange(0,BLOCK_C)
    h_base = tl.arange(0,H_CHUNK)
    # stats
    sum_x = zeros(BM)
    sum_sq = zeros(BM)
    for cb in static_range(NUM_C):
       c_offs = cb*BLOCK_C + c_base
       cmask = c_offs < C
       c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)
       mask2d = row_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
       x = load(X + b*M*C + m_offs[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], mask=mask2d, other=0)
       sum_x += sum(x)
       sum_sq += sum(x*x)
    mean = where(row_mask, sum_x / C, 0)
    var = max(sum_sq/C - mean*mean,0)
    rstd = where(row_mask, 1/sqrt(var+EPS),0)
    stats_offs = STATS + b*M*2 + m_offs
    store stats
    if HAS_MASK:
       m_mask = load(MASK + b*M + m_offs, mask=row_mask, other=0)
    else:
       m_mask = zeros(BM)
    for hb in static_range(NUM_H):
       h_offs = hb*H_CHUNK + h_base
       h_mask = h_offs < H
       h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)
       acc_l = zeros(BM,H_CHUNK)
       ...
       for cb in static_range(NUM_C):
          c_offs...
          cmask...
          c_pad...
          mask2d...
          x = load...
          lnw = load(LN_W + c_pad, mask=cmask, other=0)
          lnb = ...
          xn = (x - mean[:,None]) * rstd[:,None] * lnw[None,:] + lnb[None,:]
          xn = where(row_mask[:,None], xn, 0)
          xb = xn.to(bf16)
          w_mask = cmask[:,None] & h_mask[None,:]
          w_l = load(WL + c_pad[:,None]*H + h_pad[None,:], mask=w_mask, other=0)
          ...
          acc_l = acc_l + dot(xb,w_l)
          ...
       gl=sigmoid(acc_gl); gr=sigmoid(acc_gr)
       if HAS_MASK:

import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl

"""..."""

def _next_pow2(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()

@triton.jit
def _sigmoid(x):
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-x))

@triton.jit
def _proj_kernel(...):
    ...
```

Now, kernel parameter lists long; line breaks. Ensure trailing commas okay.

Proj kernel code in detail:

```
@triton.jit
def _proj_kernel(
    X_ptr, MASK_ptr, LN_W_ptr, LN_B_ptr,
    WL_ptr, WR_ptr, WGL_ptr, WGR_ptr,
    STATS_ptr, LT_ptr, RT_ptr,
    M, C, H, EPS,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr,
    H_CHUNK: tl.constexpr, NUM_H: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
):
    b = tl.program_id(0)
    pm = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m_raw = pm * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    row_mask = offs_m_raw < M
    m_offs = tl.where(row_mask, offs_m_raw, 0)
    c_base = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    h_base = tl.arange(0, H_CHUNK)

    sum_x = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
    sum_sq = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
    for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
        c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
        cmask = c_offs < C
        c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)
        mask2d = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
        x = tl.load(X_ptr + b * M * C + m_offs[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :], mask=mask2d, other=0.0)
        sum_x += tl.sum(x, axis=1)
        sum_sq += tl.sum(x * x, axis=1)

    mean = tl.where(row_mask, sum_x / C, 0.0)
    var = tl.maximum(sum_sq / C - mean * mean, 0.0)
    rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS), 0.0)
    stats_offs = STATS_ptr + b * M * 2 + m_offs
    tl.store(stats_offs, mean, mask=row_mask)
    tl.store(stats_offs + 1, rstd, mask=row_mask)

    if HAS_MASK:
        m_mask = tl.load(MASK_ptr + b * M + m_offs, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)
    else:
        m_mask = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)

    for hb in tl.static_range(NUM_H):
        h_offs = hb * H_CHUNK + h_base
        h_mask = h_offs < H
        h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)

        acc_l = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_gl = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_r = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_gr = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)

        for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
            cmask = c_offs < C
            c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)
            mask2d = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
            x = tl.load(X_ptr + b * M * C + m_offs[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :], mask=mask2d, other=0.0)
            lnw = tl.load(LN_W_ptr + c_pad, mask=cmask, other=0.0)
            lnb = tl.load(LN_B_ptr + c_pad, mask=cmask, other=0.0)
            xn = (x - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * lnw[None, :] + lnb[None, :]
            xn = tl.where(row_mask[:, None], xn, 0.0)
            xb = xn.to(tl.bfloat16)
            w_mask = cmask[:, None] & h_mask[None, :]
            w_l = tl.load(WL_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            w_gl = tl.load(WGL_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            w_r = tl.load(WR_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            w_gr = tl.load(WGR_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            acc_l = acc_l + tl.dot(xb, w_l)
            acc_gl = acc_gl + tl.dot(xb, w_gl)
            acc_r = acc_r + tl.dot(xb, w_r)
            acc_gr = acc_gr + tl.dot(xb, w_gr)

        gl = _sigmoid(acc_gl)
        gr = _sigmoid(acc_gr)
        if HAS_MASK:
            l_val = acc_l * gl * m_mask[:, None]
            r_val = acc_r * gr * m_mask[:, None]
        else:
            l_val = acc_l * gl
            r_val = acc_r * gr
        l_b = l_val.to(tl.bfloat16)
        r_b = r_val.to(tl.bfloat16)
        l_t = tl.trans(l_b)
        r_t = tl.trans(r_b)
        store_mask = h_mask[:, None] & row_mask[None, :]
        base_t = b * H * M
        lt_ptrs = LT_ptr + base_t + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :]
        rt_ptrs = RT_ptr + base_t + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :]
        tl.store(lt_ptrs, l_t, mask=store_mask)
        tl.store(rt_ptrs, r_t, mask=store_mask)
```
Looks good.

@triton.jit
def _outgate_kernel(X_ptr, STATS_ptr, LN_W_ptr, LN_B_ptr, WG_ptr, GATE_PTR,
                    M, C, H, EPS,
                    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr,
                    H_CHUNK: tl.constexpr, NUM_H: tl.constexpr):
    b = tl.program_id(0); pm = tl.program_id(1)
    offs_m_raw... m_offs
    c_base, h_base
    mean = tl.load(STATS_ptr + b*M*2 + m_offs, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)
    rstd = tl.load(STATS_ptr + b*M*2 + m_offs + 1, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)
    for hb:
      h_offs...
      acc = zeros(BM,H_CHUNK)
      for cb:
        ... x, lnw, lnb, xn, row where, xb, w_mask, w load, acc=acc+dot
      gate = _sigmoid(acc).to(bf16)
      gate_t = tl.trans(gate)
      store_mask = h_mask[:,None]&row_mask[None,:]
      gate_ptrs = GATE_PTR + b*H*M + h_pad[:,None]*M + m_offs[None,:]
      tl.store(gate_ptrs, gate_t, mask=store_mask)
```
`mean/rstd` rows invalid 0. xn where row false zero. Good.

Final:
```
@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(OUTT_PTR, GATE_PTR, W_OUT_PTR, LN_W_PTR, LN_B_PTR, OUT_PTR,
                  M, C, H, EPS,
                  BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr,
                  H_CHUNK: tl.constexpr, NUM_H: tl.constexpr):
    b=... pm=...
    offs_m_raw... m_offs
    h_base = arange(H_CHUNK)
    c_base = arange(BLOCK_C)
    base = b * H * M
    if NUM_H == 1:
        h_offs = h_base
        h_mask = h_offs < H
        h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)
        load_mask = h_mask[:,None] & row_mask[None,:]
        out = tl.load(OUTT_PTR + base + h_pad[:,None]*M + m_offs[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        gate = tl.load(GATE_PTR + base + h_pad[:,None]*M + m_offs[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
        mean = tl.sum(out, axis=0) / H
        sum_sq = tl.sum(out * out, axis=0)
        var = tl.maximum(sum_sq / H - mean * mean, 0.0)
        rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS), 0.0)
        lnw = tl.load(LN_W_PTR + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
        lnb = tl.load(LN_B_PTR + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
        x = (out - mean[None,:]) * rstd[None,:] * lnw[:,None] + lnb[None,:]
        x = x * gate
        x_mh = tl.trans(x.to(tl.bfloat16))
        for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs = cb*BLOCK_C + c_base
            cmask = c_offs < C
            c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)
            w_mask = h_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
            w = tl.load(W_OUT_PTR + h_pad[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
            acc = tl.dot(x_mh, w)
            store_mask = row_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
            tl.store(OUT_PTR + b*M*C + m_offs[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], acc, mask=store_mask)
    else:
        sum_x = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
        sum_sq = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
        for hb in tl.static_range(NUM_H):
            h_offs = hb*H_CHUNK + h_base
            h_mask = h_offs < H
            h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)
            load_mask = h_mask[:,None] & row_mask[None,:]
            out = tl.load(OUTT_PTR + base + h_pad[:,None]*M + m_offs[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
            sum_x += tl.sum(out, axis=0)
            sum_sq += tl.sum(out * out, axis=0)
        mean = sum_x / H
        var = tl.maximum(sum_sq / H - mean * mean, 0.0)
        rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS), 0.0)
        for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs = cb*BLOCK_C + c_base
            cmask = c_offs < C
            c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)
            acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)
            for hb in tl.static_range(NUM_H):
                h_offs = hb*H_CHUNK + h_base
                h_mask = h_offs < H
                h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)
                load_mask = h_mask[:,None] & row_mask[None,:]
                out = tl.load(OUTT_PTR + base + h_pad[:,None]*M + m_offs[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
                gate = tl.load(GATE_PTR + base + h_pad[:,None]*M + m_offs[None,:], mask=load_mask, other=0.0).to(tl.float32)
                lnw = tl.load(LN_W_PTR + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
                lnb = tl.load(LN_B_PTR + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
                x = (out - mean[None,:]) * rstd[None,:] * lnw[:,None] + lnb[None,:]
                x = x * gate
                x_mh = tl.trans(x.to(tl.bfloat16))
                w_mask = h_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
                w = tl.load(W_OUT_PTR + h_pad[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], mask=w_mask, other=0.0)
                acc = acc + tl.dot(x_mh, w)
            store_mask = row_mask[:,None] & cmask[None,:]
            tl.store(OUT_PTR + b*M*C + m_offs[:,None]*C + c_pad[None,:], acc, mask=store_mask)
```
Good.

```python
"""
Fused Triton implementation of the AlphaFold3 outgoing TriMul operation.

Pipeline:
1) Fused LayerNorm + four projections/gates, writing masked+gated L/R into
   [B, hidden, N*N] layout for efficient batched GEMM.
2) Fused out_gate projection.
3) Triton batched GEMM core:
   out[b, h, i, j] = sum_k L[b, h, i, k] * R[b, h, j, k]
4) Fused final LayerNorm, out_gate multiplication, and final projection.
"""

import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl


def _next_pow2(n: int) -> int:
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


@triton.jit
def _sigmoid(x):
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + tl.exp(-x))


@triton.jit
def _proj_kernel(
    X_ptr, MASK_ptr, LN_W_ptr, LN_B_ptr,
    WL_ptr, WR_ptr, WGL_ptr, WGR_ptr,
    STATS_ptr, LT_ptr, RT_ptr,
    M, C, H, EPS,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr,
    H_CHUNK: tl.constexpr, NUM_H: tl.constexpr,
    HAS_MASK: tl.constexpr,
):
    b = tl.program_id(0)
    pm = tl.program_id(1)

    offs_m_raw = pm * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    row_mask = offs_m_raw < M
    m_offs = tl.where(row_mask, offs_m_raw, 0)

    c_base = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    h_base = tl.arange(0, H_CHUNK)

    # LayerNorm statistics over the last dimension C.
    sum_x = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
    sum_sq = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
    for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
        c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
        cmask = c_offs < C
        c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)
        mask2d = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
        x = tl.load(
            X_ptr + b * M * C + m_offs[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :],
            mask=mask2d,
            other=0.0,
        )
        sum_x += tl.sum(x, axis=1)
        sum_sq += tl.sum(x * x, axis=1)

    mean = tl.where(row_mask, sum_x / C, 0.0)
    var = tl.maximum(sum_sq / C - mean * mean, 0.0)
    rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS), 0.0)

    stats_offs = STATS_ptr + b * M * 2 + m_offs
    tl.store(stats_offs, mean, mask=row_mask)
    tl.store(stats_offs + 1, rstd, mask=row_mask)

    if HAS_MASK:
        m_mask = tl.load(MASK_ptr + b * M + m_offs, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)
    else:
        m_mask = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)

    # For each hidden chunk, compute the four needed linear outputs.
    for hb in tl.static_range(NUM_H):
        h_offs = hb * H_CHUNK + h_base
        h_mask = h_offs < H
        h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)

        acc_l = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_gl = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_r = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)
        acc_gr = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)

        for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
            cmask = c_offs < C
            c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)
            mask2d = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]

            x = tl.load(
                X_ptr + b * M * C + m_offs[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :],
                mask=mask2d,
                other=0.0,
            )
            lnw = tl.load(LN_W_ptr + c_pad, mask=cmask, other=0.0)
            lnb = tl.load(LN_B_ptr + c_pad, mask=cmask, other=0.0)

            xn = (x - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * lnw[None, :] + lnb[None, :]
            xn = tl.where(row_mask[:, None], xn, 0.0)
            xb = xn.to(tl.bfloat16)

            w_mask = cmask[:, None] & h_mask[None, :]
            w_l = tl.load(
                WL_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :],
                mask=w_mask,
                other=0.0,
            )
            w_gl = tl.load(
                WGL_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :],
                mask=w_mask,
                other=0.0,
            )
            w_r = tl.load(
                WR_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :],
                mask=w_mask,
                other=0.0,
            )
            w_gr = tl.load(
                WGR_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :],
                mask=w_mask,
                other=0.0,
            )

            acc_l = acc_l + tl.dot(xb, w_l)
            acc_gl = acc_gl + tl.dot(xb, w_gl)
            acc_r = acc_r + tl.dot(xb, w_r)
            acc_gr = acc_gr + tl.dot(xb, w_gr)

        gl = _sigmoid(acc_gl)
        gr = _sigmoid(acc_gr)

        if HAS_MASK:
            l_val = acc_l * gl * m_mask[:, None]
            r_val = acc_r * gr * m_mask[:, None]
        else:
            l_val = acc_l * gl
            r_val = acc_r * gr

        # Store transposed: [B, H, N*N] with N*N contiguous for GEMM.
        l_b = l_val.to(tl.bfloat16)
        r_b = r_val.to(tl.bfloat16)
        l_t = tl.trans(l_b)
        r_t = tl.trans(r_b)

        store_mask = h_mask[:, None] & row_mask[None, :]
        base_t = b * H * M
        lt_ptrs = LT_ptr + base_t + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :]
        rt_ptrs = RT_ptr + base_t + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :]
        tl.store(lt_ptrs, l_t, mask=store_mask)
        tl.store(rt_ptrs, r_t, mask=store_mask)


@triton.jit
def _outgate_kernel(
    X_ptr, STATS_ptr, LN_W_ptr, LN_B_ptr, WG_ptr, GATE_PTR,
    M, C, H, EPS,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr,
    H_CHUNK: tl.constexpr, NUM_H: tl.constexpr,
):
    b = tl.program_id(0)
    pm = tl.program_id(1)

    offs_m_raw = pm * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    row_mask = offs_m_raw < M
    m_offs = tl.where(row_mask, offs_m_raw, 0)

    c_base = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    h_base = tl.arange(0, H_CHUNK)

    mean = tl.load(STATS_ptr + b * M * 2 + m_offs, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)
    rstd = tl.load(STATS_ptr + b * M * 2 + m_offs + 1, mask=row_mask, other=0.0)

    for hb in tl.static_range(NUM_H):
        h_offs = hb * H_CHUNK + h_base
        h_mask = h_offs < H
        h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)

        acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, H_CHUNK), dtype=tl.float32)

        for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
            cmask = c_offs < C
            c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)
            mask2d = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]

            x = tl.load(
                X_ptr + b * M * C + m_offs[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :],
                mask=mask2d,
                other=0.0,
            )
            lnw = tl.load(LN_W_ptr + c_pad, mask=cmask, other=0.0)
            lnb = tl.load(LN_B_ptr + c_pad, mask=cmask, other=0.0)

            xn = (x - mean[:, None]) * rstd[:, None] * lnw[None, :] + lnb[None, :]
            xn = tl.where(row_mask[:, None], xn, 0.0)
            xb = xn.to(tl.bfloat16)

            w_mask = cmask[:, None] & h_mask[None, :]
            w = tl.load(
                WG_ptr + c_pad[:, None] * H + h_pad[None, :],
                mask=w_mask,
                other=0.0,
            )

            acc = acc + tl.dot(xb, w)

        gate = _sigmoid(acc).to(tl.bfloat16)
        gate_t = tl.trans(gate)

        store_mask = h_mask[:, None] & row_mask[None, :]
        gate_ptrs = GATE_PTR + b * H * M + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :]
        tl.store(gate_ptrs, gate_t, mask=store_mask)


@triton.jit
def _core_kernel(
    L_ptr, R_ptr, O_ptr,
    N, H, M,
    BLOCK_I: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_J: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_K: tl.constexpr,
    EVEN: tl.constexpr,
):
    j = tl.program_id(0)
    i = tl.program_id(1)
    bh = tl.program_id(2)

    b = bh // H
    h = bh % H

    offs_i_raw = i * BLOCK_I + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_I)
    offs_j_raw = j * BLOCK_J + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_J)
    offs_k = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_K)

    base = (b * H + h) * M

    acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_I, BLOCK_J), dtype=tl.float32)

    if EVEN:
        offs_i = offs_i_raw
        offs_j = offs_j_raw
        l_ptrs = L_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_k[None, :]
        r_ptrs = R_ptr + base + offs_j[:, None] * N + offs_k[None, :]

        for _k in range(0, N, BLOCK_K):
            a = tl.load(l_ptrs)
            rb = tl.load(r_ptrs)
            acc = acc + tl.dot(a, tl.trans(rb))
            l_ptrs += BLOCK_K
            r_ptrs += BLOCK_K

        o_ptrs = O_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_j[None, :]
        tl.store(o_ptrs, acc.to(tl.bfloat16))
    else:
        i_mask = offs_i_raw < N
        j_mask = offs_j_raw < N
        offs_i = tl.where(i_mask, offs_i_raw, 0)
        offs_j = tl.where(j_mask, offs_j_raw, 0)

        l_ptrs = L_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_k[None, :]
        r_ptrs = R_ptr + base + offs_j[:, None] * N + offs_k[None, :]

        for k in range(0, N, BLOCK_K):
            k_offs = k + offs_k
            k_mask = k_offs < N

            a = tl.load(l_ptrs, mask=i_mask[:, None] & k_mask[None, :], other=0.0)
            rb = tl.load(r_ptrs, mask=j_mask[:, None] & k_mask[None, :], other=0.0)
            acc = acc + tl.dot(a, tl.trans(rb))

            l_ptrs += BLOCK_K
            r_ptrs += BLOCK_K

        o_ptrs = O_ptr + base + offs_i[:, None] * N + offs_j[None, :]
        tl.store(
            o_ptrs,
            acc.to(tl.bfloat16),
            mask=i_mask[:, None] & j_mask[None, :],
            other=0.0,
        )


@triton.jit
def _final_kernel(
    OUTT_PTR, GATE_PTR, W_OUT_PTR, LN_W_PTR, LN_B_PTR, OUT_PTR,
    M, C, H, EPS,
    BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_C: tl.constexpr, NUM_C: tl.constexpr,
    H_CHUNK: tl.constexpr, NUM_H: tl.constexpr,
):
    b = tl.program_id(0)
    pm = tl.program_id(1)

    offs_m_raw = pm * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)
    row_mask = offs_m_raw < M
    m_offs = tl.where(row_mask, offs_m_raw, 0)

    h_base = tl.arange(0, H_CHUNK)
    c_base = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_C)
    base = b * H * M

    # Fast path for the common case hidden_dim <= H_CHUNK (e.g. 128):
    # load the whole hidden vector once, normalize, then project.
    if NUM_H == 1:
        h_offs = h_base
        h_mask = h_offs < H
        h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)

        load_mask = h_mask[:, None] & row_mask[None, :]
        out = tl.load(
            OUTT_PTR + base + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :],
            mask=load_mask,
            other=0.0,
        ).to(tl.float32)
        gate = tl.load(
            GATE_PTR + base + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :],
            mask=load_mask,
            other=0.0,
        ).to(tl.float32)

        mean = tl.sum(out, axis=0) / H
        sum_sq = tl.sum(out * out, axis=0)
        var = tl.maximum(sum_sq / H - mean * mean, 0.0)
        rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS), 0.0)

        lnw = tl.load(LN_W_PTR + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
        lnb = tl.load(LN_B_PTR + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)

        x = (out - mean[None, :]) * rstd[None, :] * lnw[:, None] + lnb[None, :]
        x = x * gate
        x_mh = tl.trans(x.to(tl.bfloat16))

        for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
            cmask = c_offs < C
            c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)

            w_mask = h_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
            w = tl.load(
                W_OUT_PTR + h_pad[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :],
                mask=w_mask,
                other=0.0,
            )

            acc = tl.dot(x_mh, w)
            store_mask = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
            tl.store(
                OUT_PTR + b * M * C + m_offs[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :],
                acc,
                mask=store_mask,
            )
    else:
        # General path for larger hidden dims: first compute full hidden-dim
        # LN statistics, then accumulate contributions from hidden chunks.
        sum_x = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)
        sum_sq = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M,), dtype=tl.float32)

        for hb in tl.static_range(NUM_H):
            h_offs = hb * H_CHUNK + h_base
            h_mask = h_offs < H
            h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)
            load_mask = h_mask[:, None] & row_mask[None, :]
            out = tl.load(
                OUTT_PTR + base + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :],
                mask=load_mask,
                other=0.0,
            ).to(tl.float32)
            sum_x += tl.sum(out, axis=0)
            sum_sq += tl.sum(out * out, axis=0)

        mean = sum_x / H
        var = tl.maximum(sum_sq / H - mean * mean, 0.0)
        rstd = tl.where(row_mask, 1.0 / tl.sqrt(var + EPS), 0.0)

        for cb in tl.static_range(NUM_C):
            c_offs = cb * BLOCK_C + c_base
            cmask = c_offs < C
            c_pad = tl.where(cmask, c_offs, 0)

            acc = tl.zeros((BLOCK_M, BLOCK_C), dtype=tl.float32)

            for hb in tl.static_range(NUM_H):
                h_offs = hb * H_CHUNK + h_base
                h_mask = h_offs < H
                h_pad = tl.where(h_mask, h_offs, 0)
                load_mask = h_mask[:, None] & row_mask[None, :]

                out = tl.load(
                    OUTT_PTR + base + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :],
                    mask=load_mask,
                    other=0.0,
                ).to(tl.float32)
                gate = tl.load(
                    GATE_PTR + base + h_pad[:, None] * M + m_offs[None, :],
                    mask=load_mask,
                    other=0.0,
                ).to(tl.float32)

                lnw = tl.load(LN_W_PTR + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)
                lnb = tl.load(LN_B_PTR + h_pad, mask=h_mask, other=0.0)

                x = (out - mean[None, :]) * rstd[None, :] * lnw[:, None] + lnb[None, :]
                x = x * gate
                x_mh = tl.trans(x.to(tl.bfloat16))

                w_mask = h_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
                w = tl.load(
                    W_OUT_PTR + h_pad[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :],
                    mask=w_mask,
                    other=0.0,
                )

                acc = acc + tl.dot(x_mh, w)

            store_mask = row_mask[:, None] & cmask[None, :]
            tl.store(
                OUT_PTR + b * M * C + m_offs[:, None] * C + c_pad[None, :],
                acc,
                mask=store_mask,
            )


def custom_kernel(data):
    if len(data) == 3:
        input_tensor, weights, config = data
        mask = None
    else:
        input_tensor, mask, weights, config = data

    B, N, _, C = input_tensor.shape
    H = int(config.get("hidden_dim", 128))
    M = N * N
    device = input_tensor.device
    eps = float(config.get("epsilon", config.get("eps", 1e-5)))

    x = input_tensor.contiguous()
    if x.dtype != torch.float32:
        x = x.to(torch.float32)
    x = x.view(B, M, C)

    has_mask = (mask is not None) and (not config.get("nomask", False))
    if has_mask:
        mask = mask.to(torch.float32)
        if mask.shape != (B, N, N):
            if mask.numel() == B * N * N:
                mask = mask.reshape(B, N, N)
            else:
                mask = mask.expand(B, N, N)
        mask = mask.contiguous().view(B, M)
    else:
        mask = x

    def _to_t_bf16(w: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return w.t().to(torch.bfloat16).contiguous()

    ln_w = weights["norm.weight"].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    ln_b = weights["norm.bias"].to(torch.float32).contiguous()

    wl = _to_t_bf16(weights["left_proj.weight"])
    wr = _to_t_bf16(weights["right_proj.weight"])
    wgl = _to_t_bf16(weights["left_gate.weight"])
    wgr = _to_t_bf16(weights["right_gate.weight"])
    wog = _to_t_bf16(weights["out_gate.weight"])
    wout = _to_t_bf16(weights["to_out.weight"])

    ln_w2 = weights["to_out_norm.weight"].to(torch.float32).contiguous()
    ln_b2 = weights["to_out_norm.bias"].to(torch.float32).contiguous()

    bf16 = torch.bfloat16

    Lt = torch.empty((B, H, M), dtype=bf16, device=device)
    Rt = torch.empty((B, H, M), dtype=bf16, device=device)
    Stats = torch.empty((B, M, 2), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)

    BLOCK_C = 128 if C >= 128 else max(16, _next_pow2(C))
    NUM_C = triton.cdiv(C, BLOCK_C)
    H_CHUNK = min(128, max(16, _next_pow2(H)))
    NUM_H = triton.cdiv(H, H_CHUNK)

    PROJ_BM = 64
    num_m_proj = triton.cdiv(M, PROJ_BM)
    _proj_kernel[(B, num_m_proj)](
        x,
        mask if has_mask else x,
        ln_w,
        ln_b,
        wl,
        wr,
        wgl,
        wgr,
        Stats,
        Lt,
        Rt,
        M,
        C,
        H,
        eps,
        BLOCK_M=PROJ_BM,
        BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
        NUM_C=NUM_C,
        H_CHUNK=H_CHUNK,
        NUM_H=NUM_H,
        HAS_MASK=has_mask,
        num_warps=8,
        num_stages=2,
    )

    mask = None

    OUT_BM = 128
    GateT = torch.empty((B, H, M), dtype=bf16, device=device)
    num_m_out = triton.cdiv(M, OUT_BM)
    _outgate_kernel[(B, num_m_out)](
        x,
        Stats,
        ln_w,
        ln_b,
        wog,
        GateT,
        M,
        C,
        H,
        eps,
        BLOCK_M=OUT_BM,
        BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
        NUM_C=NUM_C,
        H_CHUNK=H_CHUNK,
        NUM_H=NUM_H,
        num_warps=8,
        num_stages=2,
    )

    del Stats, x

    OutT = torch.empty((B, H, M), dtype=bf16, device=device)

    if N <= 256:
        BI = 64
        BN = 64
        core_warps = 4
    else:
        BI = 128
        BN = 128
        core_warps = 8
    BK = 64

    num_i = triton.cdiv(N, BI)
    num_j = triton.cdiv(N, BN)
    even_core = (N % BI == 0) and (N % BN == 0) and (N % BK == 0)

    _core_kernel[(num_j, num_i, B * H)](
        Lt,
        Rt,
        OutT,
        N,
        H,
        M,
        BLOCK_I=BI,
        BLOCK_J=BN,
        BLOCK_K=BK,
        EVEN=even_core,
        num_warps=core_warps,
        num_stages=3,
    )

    del Lt, Rt

    OUT_BM_FINAL = 64
    out = torch.empty((B, N, N, C), dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
    num_m_final = triton.cdiv(M, OUT_BM_FINAL)
    _final_kernel[(B, num_m_final)](
        OutT,
        GateT,
        wout,
        ln_w2,
        ln_b2,
        out,
        M,
        C,
        H,
        eps,
        BLOCK_M=OUT_BM_FINAL,
        BLOCK_C=BLOCK_C,
        NUM_C=NUM_C,
        H_CHUNK=H_CHUNK,
        NUM_H=NUM_H,
        num_warps=8,
        num_stages=2,
    )

    return out
```
